Let's formulate objective exactly and gradient.

Evaluation:
```
def c_values(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1-h, full)  # length 2n-1
def c5(h,n): return float(np.max(c_values(h,n))*2/n)
```
Grad for weighted sum of c_l with weights w length 2n-1:
```
def grad_weighted(h,n,w):
    dx=2/n
    g=np.zeros(n)
    # w index k=0..2n-2 corresponds d = k-(n-1)
    center=n-1
    for k in range(2*n-1):
       wk=w[k]
       if wk <= tiny: continue
       d=k-center
       if d>=0:
          # c_d = sum_{i=0}^{n-1-d} h[i+d]*(1-h[i])
          # grad h[p] derivative: as i+d: +(1-h[i]) for p=i+d; as i: -h[i+d]
          if d==0:
             # c=∑h - h^2 derivative 1-2h
             g += wk*(1 - 2*h)
          else:
             # p as later: i=p-d, p=d..n-1: + (1-h[p-d])
             g[d:] += wk*(1 - h[:n-d])
             # p as earlier: i=p, p=0..n-d-1: - h[p+d]
             g[:n-d] -= wk*h[d:]
       else:
          e=-d
          # c_{-e}=sum_{i=e}^{n-1} h[i-e]*(1-h[i])
          # p as earlier i=p+e, p=0..n-e-1: +(1-h[p+e])
          g[:n-e] += wk*(1 - h[e:])
          # p as later i=p, p=e..n-1: -h[p-e]
          g[e:] -= wk*h[:n-e]
    return g*dx
```
For d=0, formula for d≥0 would g[0:] += (1-h[:n]) and g[:n] -=h[0:] => 1-2h okay, works if d=0 and no double? We can use same but careful lengths. For d=0: g[:] += (1-h); g[:] -= h -> 1-2h. Good.

- Soft logsum: scale=beta*c; m=max; w=exp(scale-m)/sum; objective=(m/logsum)/beta? Actually logsumexp(beta*c)=m+logsum(exp(scale-m)); objective = (logsumexp(beta*c)+?)*dx = (m + log(sum exp))/beta * dx. Gradient = ∇c * w where w=exp(scale-m)/sum.

Let's derive total sum of c over lags for general h:

Let's examine `project_feasible` robust:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(h,0,1).astype(float)
 target=n/2
 for _ in range(200):
   diff=h.sum()-target
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0: mask=h>1e-12
   else: mask=h<1-1e-12
   if mask.sum()==0: break
   h[mask]-=diff/mask.sum()
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # final small correction
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>0:
   idx=np.where((h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9))[0]
   if len(idx):
      step=diff/len(idx) if abs(diff)<0.1? else split
      h[idx]+=step; h=np.clip
   # adjust one
   if abs(target-h.sum())>1e-15:
      j=(h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9)
      if any:
        pos=np.where(h>1e-9&...)[0]
        h[pos[0]] += (target - h.sum()); h[pos[0]]=clip
 return h
```
Need be careful if diff large and mask steps cause overshoot. The iterative waterfill should converge.

Need define `optimize_sequence(h,n,deadline,light=False)`. This function top-level; uses SLSQP. It will update local best. It should not exceed time by checking before each SLSQP.

`optimize_sequence` design:
```
def optimize_sequence(h,n,deadline,light=False):
 h=project_feasible(h,n); best=eval
 bounds=[(0,1)]*n; cons=[...]
 # soft betas: if not light [20,60,150,500,1200] else [50,200]
 maxiters = [150,250,300,400,500] if n<120 else [100,150,200,250,300]
 for beta,mx in zip:
   if time.time()>deadline-20: break
   res=minimize(objc_soft,h,(n,beta), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, cons=cons, options={'maxiter':mx,'ftol':1e-12})
   h2=project_feasible(clip(res.x),n); c=eval; if c<best: h=h2; best=c; continue
 # top soft? maybe final high beta 2000 with mx 300
 # hard final: for pass in range(2): minimize(objc_hard) mx 300/200
 # topavg B=3,5 maybe if time
 return best_h,best_c
```

Let's write `project_feasible` with exact correction:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=h.sum()-target
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0: mask=h>1e-14
       else: mask=h<1.0-1e-14
       if not mask.any(): break
       adj=diff/mask.sum()
       if abs(adj)>1e-3: # avoid huge jump? but if far infeasible, still need
          # clip adjustment to keep within bounds: choose mask of adjustable
          if diff>0:
             max_avail = h[mask]
             # reduce as much as possible
             tuneable = np.sum(np.minimum(max_avail, abs(adj)))
             if tuneable <= abs(adj):
                 h[mask]-=np.minimum(max_avail,abs(adj))?
          else: ...
       else:
       h[mask]-=adj
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final correction
    for _ in range(10):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
       if not mask.any(): break
       h[mask]-=diff/mask.sum()
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
Need avoid loops bugs. Simpler waterfill with `adj=diff/mask.sum();` Usually h feasible and target within range, adj small. If input far (sum maybe out), adj can shift many below bounds; clipping causes diff remain; loop handles.

Let's implement robust:
```
for _ in range(200):
    diff = h.sum()-target
    if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
    if diff > 0:
       # need reduce
       mask = h > 1e-14
       if not mask.any(): break
       # maximum reduction without going below 0 on this mask if uniform adj: min(h[mask])
       adj = diff / mask.sum()
       if adj > h[mask].min():
           # reduce all as much as possible to 0? iterative: set those low to 0 and repeat
           h[h < 1e-14] = 0
           continue
       h[mask] -= adj
    else:
       mask = h < 1-1e-14
       if not mask.any(): break
       adj = -diff / mask.sum()
       if adj > 1 - h[mask].max():
           h[h > 1-1e-14] = 1
           continue
       h[mask] += adj
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
```
This better, but still may leave diff after saturating. Repeat. At end if diff due rounding, distribute to interior. This is fine.

Let's test with tiny h and random? We can't run but reason. For d positive index k = n-1+d: c = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-d} h[i+d]*(1-h[i]) as derived. Gradient formula: for d>0,
- p as later i=p-d: p in d..n-1 => + (1 - h[p-d])
- p as earlier i=p: p in 0..n-d-1 => - h[p+d]
In code:
```
g[d:] += w*(1 - h[:n-d])
g[:n-d] -= w*h[d:]
```
Matches.
For d<0: e=-d, c=∑_{i=e}^{n-1} h[i-e]*(1-h[i])
- p as earlier i=p+e: p=0..n-e-1 => +(1-h[p+e])
- p as later i=p: p=e..n-1 => -h[p-e]
Code:
```
g[:n-e] += w*(1 - h[e:])
g[e:] -= w*h[:n-e]
```
Matches.
For d=0: both formulas: g[:]+=1-h; g[:]-=h =>1-2h. Good.

```
def soft_obj(h,n,beta):
 c=corr_values(h,n)
 scaled=beta*c
 m=scaled.max()
 lse=m+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(scaled-m)))
 return float(lse/beta * (2/n))
```

Now, custom projected gradient function. Let's write precise.

```
def refine_subgradient(h,n,deadline,seed,K=8):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best=eval_c5(h,n)
    current=best
    best_h=h.copy()
    dx=2/n
    lr=0.05
    stall=0
    it=0
    while time.time() < deadline-10:
       c=corr_values(h,n)
       top=np.argsort(c)[-K:]
       w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[top]=1.0/K
       g=grad_c_weighted(h,n,w)*dx
       g = g - np.mean(g)
       if np.max(np.abs(g)) < 1e-12:
          # perturb
          h = project_feasible(h + rng.randn(n)*0.02, n)
          cur=eval_c5(h,n)
          stall +=1
          if stall > 50: break
          continue
       moved=False
       for factor in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 2.0]:
          step=lr*factor
          h_new = h - step*g
          h_new = quick_project(h_new,n,30)
          val=eval_c5(h_new,n)
          if val < current + 1e-12:
              h=h_new; current=val; lr*=1.05?; moved=True; break
       if not moved:
          lr*=0.8; stall+=1
       else: stall=0
       if current < best: best=current; best_h=h.copy()
       if stall > 80:
          # smooth noise
          amp=0.03+0.02*(stall//100)
          p=smooth_perturb(n,rng,it,amp)
          h_try=project_feasible(h + p,n)
          val=eval_c5(h_try,n)
          if val < current + 0.001: # accept if not too worse
             h=h_try; current=val
          else:
             # maybe try global best plus noise
             h_try=project_feasible(best_h + p,n)
             val=eval_c5(h_try,n)
             if val < current:
                h=h_try; current=val
             else:
                break # no escape
          lr=0.05; stall=0
       it +=1
    return best_h,best
```
But accepting worse can oscillate. Simpler: only accept improving moves; if stall, replace current with best + small noise and require best improvement within some tries. This may be too complex. Maybe omit subgradient to avoid bugs/time. But it could polish.

Given uncertainty, keep subgradient simple and safe: only accept if improved current; if no improvement for 50, add small noise to current and continue; keep best. It may not escape but okay.

```
def smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n
    p=np.zeros(n)
    for f in [1,2,3,4]:
       a=rng.normal(0,1)
       p += a*np.cos(f*np.pi*x)
    p -= p.mean()
    return amp*p/np.max(np.abs(p))
```
Scale max abs to amp.

Quick project:
```
def quick_project(h,n,iters=20):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   target=n/2
   for _ in range(iters):
      diff=h.sum()-target
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      if diff>0: mask=h>1e-12
      else: mask=h<1-1e-12
      if not mask.any(): break
      h[mask]-=diff/mask.sum()
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   return h
```
Same as project but fewer. Use for subgradient; final project full.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n, iters=200):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = n / 2.0
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(iters):
        diff = h.sum() - target
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h > 1e-13
            if not mask.any():
                break
            # reduce mask uniformly; if would make any <0, just clip and continue
            adj = diff / float(mask.sum())
            if adj > h[mask].min() + 1e-15:
                h[mask] -= h[mask]
                h[h <= 1e-13] = 0.0
            else:
                h[mask] -= adj
        else:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-13
            if not mask.any():
                break
            adj = -diff / float(mask.sum())
            if adj > (1.0 - h[mask].max()) + 1e-15:
                h[mask] += (1.0 - h[mask])
                h[h >= 1.0 - 1e-13] = 1.0
            else:
                h[mask] += adj
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    # final small correction
    for _ in range(20):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        mask = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if not mask.any():
            break
        h[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
If input sum far, reduction saturates many; loop will continue. If all at 0 or1 and diff remains infeasible (e.g. target outside [0,n]), not possible for feasible target. Good.

Potential issue in diff>0 branch: `h[mask] -= h[mask]` sets all mask to 0 even if target lower; then continue. Could be okay but if many to 0, sum may drop too much below target? But we only reduce by diff? If adj > min, uniform reduction would push min below 0; reducing all to 0 reduces by sum(mask) which may be > diff, causing sum below target. That can happen. Better in saturating case reduce by min to 0 and then next iteration reduce others. Let's implement:
```
if adj > h[mask].min():
    h[mask] -= h[mask].min()
    h[h < 1e-13]=0
else: h[mask]-=adj
```
This reduces only what necessary to zero min, not all. But if min small, only part of diff removed. Next loop. Good. For upward: `adj > 1 - max` => `h[mask] += (1 - h[mask].max())` (raises max to1) only enough? Actually if uniform adj exceeds room for max, raise all by room until max hits1; reduction amount = room, not all. Then next. Code:
```
room = 1.0 - h[mask].max()
h[mask] += room
h[h > 1-1e-13]=1
```
But if max not unique, raises all in mask by room, many exceed 1 and clipped; reduction sum maybe > room? Example mask values [0.9,0.5], diff small but adj >0.1 because many; raising all by 0.1 exceeds 1 for first, total increase 0.6? Clipping sets first to1, second 0.6, sum increase 0.6 > needed. Could overshoot. Better iterative augment by assigning values with max? Simpler: If adj too large, increase only the smallest? We need exact. Could use waterfill generalized but typical candidates from feasible have diff small, adj tiny. Overshoot not issue. For robust, use a simple loop adjusting one or few if diff large? Most inputs feasible with small diff. We can simplify project to assume near feasible. For random perturbations, h perturb sum close, diff small. For Fourier clipped maybe sum off but not huge. If huge, project may overshoot but subsequent loops correct (if sum below target, increase). It still converges but maybe slower. Fine.

Could use scipy optimize? no.

`grad_c_weighted` implementation:
```
def grad_c_weighted(h,n,w):
    g=np.zeros(n,float)
    center=n-1
    for k in range(2*n-1):
       wk=w[k]
       if wk==0.0: continue
       d=k-center
       if d>0:
          L=n-d
          if L>0:
             g[d:] += wk*(1.0 - h[:L])
          if L>0:
             g[:L] -= wk*h[d:]
       elif d<0:
          e=-d
          L=n-e
          if L>0:
             g[:L] += wk*(1.0 - h[e:])
             g[e:] -= wk*h[:L]
       else:
          g += wk*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
    return g
```
For d=0, use efficient. For d±, could vector lengths zero if d=n? Not possible.

`obj_soft`:
```
def obj_soft(h,n,beta):
   c=corr_values(h,n)
   s=beta*c
   m=np.max(s)
   z=s-m
   se=np.exp(z)
   return float((m + np.log(np.sum(se)))/beta * (2.0/n))
```
If beta very large and c values negative? c nonnegative? h(1-h) nonnegative? Yes c sums nonnegative. So m nonnegative. Good. If beta=0 no. We won't call zero.

`grad_soft`: similar.

`obj_top`:
```
c=corr; B=min(B,len(c)); idx=np.argpartition(c, -B)[-B:]? Use argsort for simplicity. return c[idx].mean()*dx
```
`np.argpartition` faster but need top B values only; mean. Argsort okay for n~200. Use `idx=np.argsort(c)[-B:]`.

`grad_top`: w zeros; w[idx]=1.0/B; return grad_c_weighted(h,n,w)*dx.

Potential issue: Top objective non-smooth at ties. okay.

`refine_slsqp` details:
```
def refine_slsqp(h,n,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best=compute_c5(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_fun,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    ftol=1e-10
    if n<=100:
       stages=[(20,140),(80,200),(250,260),(800,320)]
       top_b=[(3,70),(5,70)]
       hard_m=150
       final_m=250
    elif n<=160:
       stages=[(30,100),(100,140),(300,180),(1000,220)]
       top_b=[(3,50),(5,50)]
       hard_m=100
       final_m=160
    else:
       stages=[(40,70),(150,90),(500,110),(1500,130)]
       top_b=[(3,40),(5,40)]
       hard_m=70
       final_m=100
    for beta,mx in stages:
       if time.time() > deadline-20: break
       try: res=minimize(obj_soft,h,jac=grad_soft,args=(n,beta),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':mx,'ftol':ftol,'disp':False})
       except: continue
       h_try=project_feasible(np.clip(res.x,0,1),n)
       val=compute_c5(h_try,n)
       if val < best - 1e-15:
          h=h_try; best=val
    for B,mx in top_b:
       ... obj_top grad_top
    # hard pass
    if time...:
       res=minimize(obj_hard,... maxiter=hard_m)
       update
    # symmetrize try
    h_sym=symmetrize(h,n)
    v=compute_c5(h_sym,n); if v<best: h=h_sym; best=v
    # final soft high beta and hard
    if n<=150:
       stages2=[(2000,200)] maybe
    ...
    return h,best
```
Could do final hard after sym. Need avoid too many repeated SLSQP; time.

`refine_subgradient` after SLSQP? Or before? Subgradient may find direction that SLSQP misses. Use with h from refine_slsqp; top average K=8. But if SLSQP already converged, subgrad may not improve. Could help. Time moderate. Use only if remaining >120 and n not too large. We'll include.

But subgradient with projection to feasible can be slow if 30 iters each trial. Use quick. Step dirs: since equality, gradient projection. Need choose top K default 8. For n=95, dx .021, g components maybe. Step lr maybe 0.05. We can adapt: use objective step tolerance. Let's implement safer:
```
def refine_subgradient(h,n,deadline,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best=compute_c5(h,n); current=best
    h_cur=h.copy(); best_h=h.copy()
    lr=0.03
    K=8
    stall=0
    while time.time() < deadline-15:
       c=corr_values(h_cur,n)
       top=np.argsort(c)[-K:]
       w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[top]=1.0/len(top)
       g=grad_c_weighted(h_cur,n,w)*(2.0/n)
       g=g-g.mean()
       norm=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if norm ==0: break
       # normalize direction to unit max to use lr as h step
       d = -g / norm  # max 1
       improved=False
       for s in [lr, lr*0.5, lr*0.25, lr*2.0]:
          hn=quick_project(h_cur + s*d, n, 15)
          val=compute_c5(hn,n)
          if val < current - 1e-15:
             h_cur=hn; current=val; improved=True; break
       if improved:
          if current < best: best=current; best_h=h_cur.copy()
          stall=0
          lr=min(0.1, lr*1.05)
       else:
          lr*=0.8
          stall+=1
       if stall > 40:
          # perturb current and best
          amp=0.02 + 0.01*(stall//50)
          p1=smooth_perturb(n,rng,amp)
          try1=quick_project(h_cur+p1,n,25)
          val1=compute_c5(try1,n)
          if val1 < current + 1e-4:
             h_cur=try1; current=val1
          else:
             p2=smooth_perturb(n,rng,amp)
             try2=quick_project(best_h+p2,n,25)
             val2=compute_c5(try2,n)
             if val2 < current + 1e-4:
                h_cur=try2; current=val2
             else:
                # maybe accept best if no movement
                if val1 < best + 1e-3: ...
          lr=0.03; stall=0
          if stall? to break after several fails
       # optional break if lr too small and stall high
       if lr < 1e-5 and stall > 100: break
    return best_h,best
```
The accept worse by 1e-4 may temporarily worsen; aim keep best. Okay.

However using `quick_project` with only 15 iterations may leave sum off; compute_c5 might evaluate infeasible? We should project feasible enough. Use `quick_project(..., iters=30)`. Fourier search details. To avoid too slow, use fewer starts and M. Let's write:
```
def fourier_search(base_h, base_n, n, M, starts, deadline, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    dx=2.0/n
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*dx
    freq=np.pi*np.arange(1,M+1)
    C=np.cos(np.outer(x,freq))
    best_h=None; best_val=np.inf
    # initial a list
    base=interp_to_n(base_h, base_n, n)
    base=symmetrize(base,n)
    u=base-0.5
    a0=dx*(C.T @ u)
    # triangle
    ht=np.minimum(x, 2.0-x) - 0.5
    a_tri=dx*(C.T @ ht)
    # a few fixed
    inits=[a0, a_tri.copy()]
    a1=np.zeros(M); a1[0]=-0.35; inits.append(a1)
    a2=np.zeros(M); a2[0]=-0.25; a2[1]=0.15; inits.append(a2)
    a3=np.zeros(M); a3[0]=-0.15; a3[1]=0.2; a3[2]=-0.05; inits.append(a3)
    for s in range(starts):
       if time.time() > deadline - 40: break
       if s < len(inits): a=inits[s]*1.0
       else:
          a=0.2*rng.normal(size=M); a[0]-=0.1
       # optional clamp coefficients to manage range? not.
       try:
          res=minimize(obj_coeff, a, args=(C,n,50.0), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':300,'xatol':1e-6,'fatol':1e-8,'disp':False})
          a_best=res.x
       except:
          a_best=a
       h=coeff_to_h(a_best,C,n)
       val=compute_c5(h,n)
       if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_h=h
       # also evaluate initial a raw maybe
       h0=coeff_to_h(a,C,n); v0=compute_c5(h0,n); if v0<best_val update
    return best_h, best_val
```

```
h=project_feasible(h,n,iters=25)
return obj_soft(h,n,beta) + 10*viol*viol
```

We'll implement `refine_subgradient` conservatively:
```
def refine_subgradient(h,n,deadline,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best=compute_c5(h,n); current=best
    best_h=h.copy()
    lr=0.04
    K=8
    stall=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 10:
       c=corr_values(h,n)
       top=np.argsort(c)[-K:]
       w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[top]=1.0/K
       g=grad_c_weighted(h,n,w)*(2.0/n)
       g=g-g.mean()
       norm=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if norm <= 1e-13:
          break
       d=-g/norm
       improved=False
       for s in (lr, 0.5*lr, 0.25*lr):
           h_try=project_feasible(h + s*d, n, iters=30)
           val=compute_c5(h_try,n)
           if val < current - 1e-15:
               h=h_try; current=val; improved=True; break
       if improved:
           if current < best:
              best=current; best_h=h.copy()
           stall=0
           if lr < 0.08: lr*=1.03
       else:
           stall += 1
           lr *= 0.85
           if lr < 1e-5:
              break
           if stall % 20 == 0:
              amp=0.02 + 0.005*(stall//20)
              p=smooth_perturb(n,rng,amp)
              h_try=project_feasible(h+p,n,iters=30)
              val=compute_c5(h_try,n)
              if val < current + 1e-4:
                  h=h_try; current=val
              else:
                  p=smooth_perturb(n,rng,amp)
                  h_try=project_feasible(best_h+p,n,iters=30)
                  val=compute_c5(h_try,n)
                  if val < current + 1e-4:
                      h=h_try; current=val
              lr=max(lr,0.02)
              stall=0
       if stall > 80:
          break
    return best_h,best
```
The current may accept worse by 1e-4; but best remains. Good.

Potential issue: `project_feasible(..., iters=30)` call syntax: default third positional iters. Our function signature `project_feasible(h, n, iters=200)`, okay.

```
def refine_slsqp(h,n,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best=compute_c5(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_fun,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    if n <= 100:
        stages=[(20,120),(80,160),(250,200),(800,240)]
        top_stages=[(3,40),(5,50)]
        hard_m=80
        final_stage=(2000,200)
    elif n <= 180:
        stages=[(30,80),(100,100),(300,120),(1000,140)]
        top_stages=[(3,30),(5,40)]
        hard_m=60
        final_stage=(1500,120)
    else:
        stages=[(40,60),(150,70),(500,80),(1500,90)]
        top_stages=[(3,25),(5,35)]
        hard_m=50
        final_stage=(1000,80)
    for beta,mx in stages:
       if time.time() > deadline - 15: break
       try:
          res=minimize(obj_soft,h,args=(n,beta),jac=grad_soft,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':mx,'ftol':1e-10,'disp':False})
       except Exception:
          continue
       hx=project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float),n)
       val=compute_c5(hx,n)
       if val < best - 1e-15:
           h=hx; best=val
    for B,mx in top_stages:
       ... obj_top/grad_top ftol 1e-9
    if time.time() <= deadline-20:
       res=minimize(obj_hard,... hard_m)
       update
    # sym
    hs=symmetrize(h,n); vs=compute_c5(hs,n); if vs<best: h=hs; best=vs
    # final soft if time
    if time.time() <= deadline-20:
       beta,mx=final_stage; minimize soft...
    # final hard one more?
    if time.time() <= deadline-15:
       res=minimize(obj_hard,... hard_m//2? no maybe final hard 60)
    return project_feasible(h,n), compute_c5(...), n
```

Let's write `fourier_search` carefully:
```
def fourier_search(base_h, base_n, n, M, starts, deadline, seed):
    if time.time() > deadline - 30:
        return None, np.inf, n
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    dx=2.0/n
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*dx
    freq=np.pi*np.arange(1,M+1)
    C=np.cos(np.outer(x,freq))
    base=interp_to_n(base_h,base_n,n)
    base=symmetrize(base,n)
    a_fit=dx*(C.T.dot(base-0.5))
    ht=np.minimum(x, 2.0-x) - 0.5
    a_tri=dx*(C.T.dot(ht))
    inits=[]
    for aa in (a_fit, a_tri):
       # scale to avoid wild if fit weird? clip coefficients to [-1,1]
       aa=np.clip(aa,-1.0,1.0)
       inits.append(aa)
    a_fixed1=np.zeros(M); a_fixed1[0]=-0.35
    a_fixed2=np.zeros(M); a_fixed2[0]=-0.2; a_fixed2[1]=0.12
    a_fixed3=np.zeros(M); a_fixed3[0]=-0.1; a_fixed3[1]=0.18; if M>2: a_fixed3[2]=-0.05
    inits.extend(...)
    rng_inits = [np.clip(0.25*rng.normal(size=M),-1,1) for _ in range(3)] # list comprehension okay no closure? It's fine not function; but maybe no lambdas. Use loop.
    all_inits = inits + rng_inits
    for idx in range(min(starts, len(all_inits))):
       if time.time() > deadline - 25: break
       a0=all_inits[idx].copy()
     # evaluate raw
       h0=coeff_to_h(a0,C,n); v0=compute_c5(h0,n)
       best...
       # optimize
       beta=40.0 if idx==0 else 25.0
       try:
          res=minimize(obj_coeff,a0,args=(C,n,beta),method='Powell',options={'maxiter':80,'ftol':1e-8,'disp':False})
          a_opt=res.x
          ho=coeff_to_h(a_opt,C,n); vo=...
       except: a_opt=a0
       update
       # maybe local refine? not here
    return best_h,best_c,n
```
`C.T.dot` works.

```
def slsqp_optimize(fun,jac,x0,args,n,bounds,cons,mx,ftol):
    try:
       res=minimize(fun,x0,jac=jac,args=args,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':mx,'ftol':ftol,'disp':False})
       hx=project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float),n,iters=100)
       return hx
    except Exception:
       return None
```

Detailed `run` code with indentation. Let's write carefully:

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = float(budget_s)
    global_deadline = t0 + total - 30.0
    if global_deadline < t0 + 5.0:
        global_deadline = t0 + total - 1.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed) % (2**31-1))
    try:
        init = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n0 = int(len(init))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 96
        init = np.full(n0, 0.5)
    if n0 < 16 or n0 > 300 or init.size != n0:
        if init.size > 0:
            n0 = 96
            init = interp_to_n(init, int(init.size), 96)
        else:
            n0 = 96
            init = np.full(96, 0.5)
    init = project_feasible(init, n0, iters=100)
    best_h = init
    best_n = n0
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    # parent refine
    desired = max(60.0, 0.22 * total)
    local = min(global_deadline, time.time() + desired)
    if time.time() < local - 5.0:
        h1, c1, n1 = refine_slsqp(best_h, best_n, local)
        if n1 != best_n or c1 < best_c:
            if n1 != best_n:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, n1
            else:
                best_h, best_c = h1, c1
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def corr_values(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def compute_c5(h, n):
    c = corr_values(h, n)
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def project_feasible(h, n, iters=200):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.nan_to_num(h)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(int(iters)):
        diff = h.sum() - target
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h > 1e-13
            if not mask.any():
                break
            adj = diff / float(mask.sum())
            if adj > float(h[mask].min()) + 1e-15:
                h[mask] -= float(h[mask].min())
                h[h <= 1e-13] = 0.0
            else:
                h[mask] -= adj
        else:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-13
            if not mask.any():
                break
            adj = -diff / float(mask.sum())
            room = 1.0 - float(h[mask].max())
            if adj > room + 1e-15:
                h[mask] += room
                h[h >= 1.0 - 1e-13] = 1.0
            else:
                h[mask] += adj
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        mask = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if mask.any():
            h[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            if diff > 0.0:
                idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))

    return h


def grad_c_weighted(h, n, w):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1
    ln = 2 * n - 1
    for k in range(ln):
        wk = w[k]
        if wk == 0.0:
            continue
        d = k - center
        if d == 0:
            g += wk * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif d > 0:
            L = n - d
            if L > 0:
                g[d:] += wk * (1.0 - h[:L])
                g[:L] -= wk * h[d:]
        else:
            e = -d
            L = n - e
            if L > 0:
                g[:L] += wk * (1.0 - h[e:])
                g[e:] -= wk * h[:L]
    return g


def obj_soft(h, n, beta):
    c = corr_values(h, n)
    s = beta * c
    m = float(np.max(s))
    z = s - m
    return float((m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z)))) / beta * (2.0 / float(n)))


def obj_soft_gradient(h, n, beta):
    c = corr_values(h, n)
    s = beta * c
    m = float(np.max(s))
    w = np.exp(s - m)
    w /= w.sum()
    return grad_c_weighted(h, n, w) * (2.0 / float(n))


def obj_hard(h, n):
    c = corr_values(h, n)
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def obj_hard_gradient(h, n):
    c = corr_values(h, n)
    idx = int(np.argmax(c))
    w = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=float)
    w[idx] = 1.0
    return grad_c_weighted(h, n, w) * (2.0 / float(n))


def obj_top(h, n, B):
    c = corr_values(h, n)
    B = min(int(B), len(c))
    idx = np.argsort(c)[-B:]
    return float(np.mean(c[idx]) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def obj_top_gradient(h, n, B):
    c = corr_values(h, n)
    B = min(int(B), len(c))
    idx = np.argsort(c)[-B:]
    w = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=float)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(B)
    return grad_c_weighted(h, n, w) * (2.0 / float(n))


def sum_eq_fun(h, n):
    return float(np.sum(h) - float(n) / 2.0)


def sum_eq_jac(h, n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def slsqp_optimize(fun, jac, x0, args, n, bounds, cons, maxiter, ftol):
    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun,
            x0,
            jac=jac,
            args=args,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": float(ftol), "disp": False},
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return None
        return project_feasible(x, n, iters=100)
    except Exception:
        return None


def cap_maxiter_by_time(n, mx, deadline):
    avail = float(deadline) - time.time() - 10.0
    if avail <= 5.0:
        return 0
    per_iter = 0.002 * float(n) + 0.10
    allow = int(avail / per_iter)
    if allow > int(mx):
        return int(mx)
    return max(0, allow)


def refine_slsqp(h, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n, iters=120)
    best = compute_c5(h, n)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_eq_fun, "jac": sum_eq_jac, "args": (n,)}]

    if n <= 100:
        stages = [(20, 90), (70, 120), (220, 160), (800, 200)]
        top_stages = [(3, 40), (5, 50)]
        hard_m = 70
        final_beta = 2200
        final_m = 140
    elif n <= 180:
        stages = [(30, 70), (100, 90), (300, 110), (1000, 130)]
        top_stages = [(3, 30), (5, 40)]
        hard_m = 55
        final_beta = 1500
        final_m = 100
    else:
        stages = [(40, 55), (150, 65), (500, 75), (1500, 85)]
        top_stages = [(3, 25), (5, 35)]
        hard_m = 45
        final_beta = 1000
        final_m = 70

    for beta, mx in stages:
        mx2 = cap_maxiter_by_time(n, mx, deadline)
        if mx2 <= 10:
            continue
        hx = slsqp_optimize(obj_soft, obj_soft_gradient, h, (n, beta), n, bounds, cons, mx2, 1e-10)
        if hx is None:
            continue
        val = compute_c5(hx, n)
        if val < best - 1e-15:
            h = hx
            best = val

    for B, mx in top_stages:
        mx2 = cap_maxiter_by_time(n, mx, deadline)
        if mx2 <= 10:
            continue
        hx = slsqp_optimize(obj_top, obj_top_gradient, h, (n, B), n, bounds, cons, mx2, 1e-9)
        if hx is None:
            continue
        val = compute_c5(hx, n)
        if val < best - 1e-15:
            h = hx
            best = val

    mx2 = cap_maxiter_by_time(n, hard_m, deadline)
    if mx2 > 10:
        hx = slsqp_optimize(obj_hard, obj_hard_gradient, h, (n,), n, bounds, cons, mx2, 1e-10)
        if hx is not None:
            val = compute_c5(hx, n)
            if val < best - 1e-15:
                h = hx
                best = val

    hs = project_feasible(0.5 * (h + h[::-1]), n, iters=60)
    vs = compute_c5(hs, n)
    if vs < best - 1e-15:
        h = hs
        best = vs

    mx2 = cap_maxiter_by_time(n, final_m, deadline)
    if mx2 > 10:
        hx = slsqp_optimize(obj_soft, obj_soft_gradient, h, (n, final_beta), n, bounds, cons, mx2, 1e-11)
        if hx is not None:
            val = compute_c5(hx, n)
            if val < best - 1e-15:
                h = hx
                best = val

    hs = project_feasible(0.5 * (h + h[::-1]), n, iters=60)
    vs = compute_c5(hs, n)
    if vs < best - 1e-15:
        h = hs
        best = vs

    mx2 = cap_maxiter_by_time(n, max(20, hard_m // 2), deadline)
    if mx2 > 10:
        hx = slsqp_optimize(obj_hard, obj_hard_gradient, h, (n,), n, bounds, cons, mx2, 1e-11)
        if hx is not None:
            val = compute_c5(hx, n)
            if val < best - 1e-15:
                h = hx
                best = val

    h = project_feasible(h, n, iters=120)
    best = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, best, n


def smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * dx
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        p += float(rng.normal()) * np.cos(f * np.pi * x)
    p -= p.mean()
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(p)))
    if mx > 0.0:
        p = (float(amp) * p) / mx
    p -= p.mean()
    return p


def refine_subgradient(h, n, deadline, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))
    h = project_feasible(h, n, iters=100)
    best = compute_c5(h, n)
    current = best
    best_h = h.copy()

    lr = 0.035
    K = int(min(16, max(4, n // 12 + 2)))
    stall = 0

    while time.time() < float(deadline) - 10.0:
        c = corr_values(h, n)
        top = np.argsort(c)[-K:]
        w = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=float)
        w[top] = 1.0 / float(K)
        g = grad_c_weighted(h, n, w) * (2.0 / float(n))
        g = g - g.mean()
        norm = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if norm <= 1e-13:
            break
        d = -g / norm

        improved = False
        for s in (lr, 0.5 * lr, 0.25 * lr):
            h_try = project_feasible(h + s * d, n, iters=30)
            val = compute_c5(h_try, n)
            if val < current - 1e-15:
                h = h_try
                current = val
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if current < best:
                best = current
                best_h = h.copy()
            if lr < 0.08:
                lr *= 1.03
        else:
            stall += 1
            lr *= 0.85
            if lr < 1e-5:
                break
            if stall % 20 == 0:
                amp = 0.018 + 0.004 * float(stall // 20)
                p1 = smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp)
                h_try = project_feasible(h + p1, n, iters=40)
                val_try = compute_c5(h_try, n)
                if val_try < current + 1e-4:
                    h = h_try
                    current = val_try
                else:
                    p2 = smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp)
                    h_try = project_feasible(best_h + p2, n, iters=40)
                    val_try = compute_c5(h_try, n)
                    if val_try < current + 1e-4:
                        h = h_try
                        current = val_try
                lr = max(lr, 0.02)
                stall = 0

        if stall > 90:
            break

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n, iters=100)
    best = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best, n


def interp_to_n(h, n_old, n_new):
    if int(n_old) == int(n_new):
        return project_feasible(h, int(n_new), iters=80)
    x_old = (np.arange(int(n_old), dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_old))
    x_new = (np.arange(int(n_new), dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_new))
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return project_feasible(h_new, int(n_new), iters=80)


def symmetrize(h, n):
    h2 = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(h2, int(n), iters=80)


def coeff_to_h(a, C, n):
    h_raw = 0.5 + C.dot(a)
    h_raw = np.clip(h_raw, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h_raw, int(n), iters=120)


def obj_coeff(a, C, n, beta):
    h_raw = 0.5 + C.dot(a)
    viol_pos = max(0.0, float(np.max(h_raw)) - 1.0)
    viol_neg = max(0.0, float(-np.min(h_raw)))
    viol = viol_pos + viol_neg
    h = project_feasible(np.clip(h_raw, 0.0, 1.0), int(n), iters=10)
    return obj_soft(h, int(n), beta) + 20.0 * viol * viol


def fourier_search(base_h, base_n, n, M, starts, deadline, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))
    if time.time() > float(deadline) - 30.0:
        return None, np.inf, int(n)

    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    x = (np.arange(int(n), dtype=float) + 0.5) * dx
    freq = np.pi * np.arange(1, int(M) + 1, dtype=float)
    C = np.cos(np.outer(x, freq))

    base = interp_to_n(base_h, int(base_n), int(n))
    base = symmetrize(base, int(n))

    inits = []
    a_fit = dx * (C.T.dot(base - 0.5))
    a_fit = np.clip(a_fit, -1.2, 1.2)
    inits.append(a_fit.copy())

    tri = np.minimum(x, 2.0 - x) - 0.5
    a_tri = dx * (C.T.dot(tri))
    a_tri = np.clip(a_tri, -1.2, 1.2)
    inits.append(a_tri.copy())

    a1 = np.zeros(M, dtype=float)
    a1[0] = -0.35
    inits.append(a1.copy())

    a2 = np.zeros(M, dtype=float)
    a2[0] = -0.20
    if M > 1:
        a2[1] = 0.12
    inits.append(a2.copy())

    a3 = np.zeros(M, dtype=float)
    a3[0] = -0.08
    if M > 1:
        a3[1] = 0.16
    if M > 2:
        a3[2] = -0.04
    inits.append(a3.copy())

    for _ in range(3):
        ar = 0.25 * rng.normal(size=M)
        ar[0] -= 0.04
        inits.append(np.clip(ar, -1.0, 1.0))

    best_h = None
    best_c = np.inf

    for idx in range(min(int(starts), len(inits))):
        if time.time() > float(deadline) - 25.0:
            break
        a0 = inits[idx].copy()

        h0 = coeff_to_h(a0, C, int(n))
        v0 = compute_c5(h0, int(n))
        if v0 < best_c:
            best_c = v0
            best_h = h0.copy()

        beta = 40.0 if idx == 0 else 25.0
        maxit = 35 if idx > 1 else 45
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj_coeff,
                a0,
                args=(C, int(n), beta),
                method="Powell",
                options={"maxiter": int(maxit), "ftol": 1e-8, "disp": False},
            )
            a_opt = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_opt)):
                a_opt = a0
        except Exception:
            a_opt = a0

        h_opt = coeff_to_h(a_opt, C, int(n))
        v_opt = compute_c5(h_opt, int(n))
        if v_opt < best_c:
            best_c = v_opt
            best_h = h_opt.copy()

    if best_h is None:
        return None, np.inf, int(n)
    return project_feasible(best_h, int(n), iters=120), compute_c5(best_h, int(n)), int(n)


def update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, int(n), iters=100)
    c = compute_c5(h, int(n))
    if c < best_c - 1e-15:
        return h, c, int(n)
    return best_h, best_c, int(best_n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = float(budget_s)
    global_deadline = t0 + total - 30.0
    if global_deadline < t0 + 5.0:
        global_deadline = t0 + total - 1.0

    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))

    try:
        init = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        n0 = int(len(init))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 96
        init = np.full(n0, 0.5)

    if n0 < 16 or n0 > 300 or init.size != n0:
        if init.size > 0:
            n0 = 96
            init = interp_to_n(init, int(init.size), 96)
        else:
            n0 = 96
            init = np.full(96, 0.5)

    init = project_feasible(init, n0, iters=100)
    best_h = init
    best_n = int(n0)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    # Strong local refinement of the parent construction.
    local = min(global_deadline, time.time() + max(60.0, 0.22 * total))
    if time.time() < local - 5.0:
        h1, c1, n1 = refine_slsqp(best_h, best_n, local)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, h1, n1)

    # Non-smooth top-lag subgradient polish.
    if time.time() < global_deadline - 240.0:
        local = min(global_deadline - 240.0, time.time() + min(90.0, 0.07 * total))
        if time.time() < local - 3.0:
            h1, c1, n1 = refine_subgradient(best_h, best_n, local, int(seed) + 1)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, h1, n1)

    # Small smooth restarts around the best parent basin.
    if time.time() < global_deadline - 300.0:
        for attempt in range(2):
            if time.time() > global_deadline - 280.0:
                break
            amp = 0.035 + 0.03 * float(attempt)
            hp = project_feasible(best_h + smooth_perturb(best_n, rng, amp), best_n, iters=60)
            local = min(global_deadline - 260.0, time.time() + min(60.0, 0.05 * total))
            if time.time() < local - 5.0:
                h1, c1, n1 = refine_slsqp(hp, best_n, local)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, h1, n1)

    # Low-dimensional Fourier-shaped global search at a useful resolution.
    if time.time() < global_deadline - 260.0:
        n4 = 128
        if best_n > 200:
            n4 = 160
        starts = 6 if total > 1000.0 else 4
        local = min(global_deadline - 200.0, time.time() + min(170.0, 0.14 * total))
        h4, c4, n4 = fourier_search(best_h, best_n, n4, 5, starts, local, int(seed) + 17)
        if h4 is not None and c4 < best_c + 0.008:
            local2 = min(global_deadline - 150.0, time.time() + min(110.0, 0.09 * total))
            if time.time() < local2 - 5.0:
                h4r, c4r, n4r = refine_slsqp(h4, n4, local2)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, h4r, n4r)

    # Try other discretizations and mildly perturbed interpolants.
    done_ns = []
    done_ns.append(int(best_n))
    done_ns.append(int(n0))
    done_ns = list(set(done_ns))

    cand = []
    cand.append(int(best_n))
    if best_n + 1 <= 300:
        cand.append(best_n + 1)
    if best_n - 1 >= 16:
        cand.append(best_n - 1)
    for fixed_n in (96, 100, 112, 128, 144, 160, 192, 224, 256):
        cand.append(fixed_n)

    n_list = []
    for val in cand:
        iv = int(val)
        if 16 <= iv <= 300 and iv not in n_list:
            n_list.append(iv)

    alt_start = time.time()
    alt_budget = min(0.25 * total, max(60.0, total - 750.0))

    for n in n_list:
        if time.time() - t0 > total - 230.0:
            break
        if time.time() > alt_start + alt_budget:
            break
        if n in done_ns:
            continue
        if time.time() >= global_deadline - 90.0:
            break

        h0 = interp_to_n(best_h, best_n, n)
        hs = symmetrize(h0, n)
        if compute_c5(hs, n) < compute_c5(h0, n):
            h0 = hs

        for attempt in range(2):
            if time.time() > alt_start + alt_budget:
                break
            hp = h0
            if attempt == 1:
                hp = project_feasible(h0 + smooth_perturb(n, rng, 0.03), n, iters=60)
            local = min(global_deadline - 90.0, time.time() + 65.0)
            if time.time() < local - 5.0:
                h2, c2, n2 = refine_slsqp(hp, n, local)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, h2, n2)

        done_ns.append(int(n))
        if int(best_n) not in done_ns:
            done_ns.append(int(best_n))

    # Final polishing of the incumbent.
    if time.time() < global_deadline - 30.0:
        local = min(global_deadline, time.time() + max(30.0, 0.07 * total))
        if time.time() < local - 5.0:
            h1, c1, n1 = refine_slsqp(best_h, best_n, local)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, h1, n1)

        if time.time() < global_deadline - 15.0:
            local2 = min(global_deadline, time.time() + max(10.0, 0.02 * total))
            if time.time() < local2 - 3.0:
                h2, c2, n2 = refine_subgradient(best_h, best_n, local2, int(seed) + 88)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, h2, n2)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n, iters=200)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    try:
        ev = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h, best_n)
        if ev is not None:
            ev = float(ev)
            if 0.0 < ev < 1.0 and best_c - ev < 1e-4 and ev - best_c < 1e-4:
                best_c = min(best_c, ev)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
